# Preliminary Hearing, Day 3 — Transcript (July 8, 2026)
Continues [[2026-07-06 - Preliminary Hearing Day 1 Transcript|Day 1]] and [[2026-07-07 - Preliminary Hearing Day 2 Transcript|Day 2]] · *State of Utah v. Tyler James Robinson*, Case No. 251403576 · before [[Judge Tony Graf]] · video: https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=szsmK2Ul9XY

> 🎥 **Watch the source recording** — the timestamps below deep-link into this video.

![](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=szsmK2Ul9XY)

> Speaker labels come from automated diarization, cleaned and re-attributed by role — verify against the video before quoting a specific person by name.

## Summary
**What happened:** Day 3 (~3.5 hours) continued the State’s case before [[Judge Tony Graf]] and again **ended mid-hearing**, recessed to **Day 4 (July 9, 9:00 a.m.)**. The State called one live witness — **[[Brian Davis|Agent Brian Davis]]** (Utah SBI case agent and affiant on the probable-cause affidavit), examined on direct by prosecutor **Lauren Hunt** — who walked through the investigation and laid the foundation for the **roommate evidence**: **[[Lance Twiggs]]** was interviewed twice (Sept. 12, and again later with counsel), gave a **buccal (DNA) swab**, and was granted **use immunity**, with both interviews recorded. The bulk of the day was **legal argument** over admitting, under **Utah Rule of Evidence 1102** (reliable hearsay at a preliminary hearing), **Twiggs’s recorded interview** in lieu of live testimony, together with the **Discord / text-message thread (Exhibit 16)** and the **[[The Keyboard Note|keyboard-note]] confession** — offered for **motive** and probable cause. The **defense (Novak / Burt) objected** on **Confrontation Clause** grounds (citing *Archuleta*, *Estes v. Texas*, *Rideau v. Louisiana*), Rule 1102, best-evidence, redaction, and fair-trial / pretrial-publicity concerns; **media counsel** argued publication and sealing. The court allowed some material in for the limited **probable-cause** purpose (with redactions and jury-pool caution) but **reserved** the fuller Confrontation question, asked the **defense to submit a brief/memorandum**, and **recessed**. **No bind-over.** Full defense-side analysis: [[2026-07-08 - Preliminary Hearing Day 3|Day 3 Summary & Legal Review]].

> This is a **preliminary hearing**, not a trial — the standard is **probable cause**, and **reliable hearsay is admissible** (Utah Const. Art. I §12; Utah R. Evid. 1102). Robinson is **presumed innocent**.
